Method: 提出预测-自适应框架，核心是Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)，将物理变量作为独立token处理，显式实现跨变量注意力，结构上分离短时域惯性依赖与长时域气动效应。为应对部署时分布偏移，Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)通过递归最小二乘法在潜空间进行快速线性自适应，保留离线非线性表示而不产生过高计算开销。自适应残差预测直接集成到残差补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在承受未知载荷的空中机械臂上进行真实世界实验，相比最先进的学习基线，展示了更高的预测保真度、更快的扰动衰减和更优的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的FDT-LRA框架通过显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，结合快速潜空间自适应，有效解决了自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学建模问题，在真实场景中实现了高性能的实时控制。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations. Conventional analytical dynamic models rely on fixed parametric structures, while static data-driven model assume stationary dynamics and degrade under configuration changes and payload variations. Moreover, existing learning architectures do not explicitly factorize cross-variable coupling and multi-scale temporal effects, conflating instantaneous inertial dynamics with long-horizon regime evolution. We propose a predictive-adaptive framework for real-time residual modeling and compensation in AAMs. The core of this framework is the Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT), which treats physical variables as independent tokens. This design enables explicit cross-variable attention while structurally separating short-horizon inertial dependencies from long-horizon aerodynamic effects. To address deployment-time distribution shifts, a Latent Residual Adapter (LRA) performs rapid linear adaptation in the latent space via Recursive Least Squares, preserving the offline nonlinear representation without prohibitive computational overhead. The adapted residual forecast is directly integrated into a residual-compensated adaptive controller. Real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator subjected to unseen payloads demonstrate higher prediction fidelity, accelerated disturbance attenuation, and superior closed-loop tracking precision compared to state-of-the-art learning baselines, all while maintaining strict real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [188] [Chunk-Boundary Artifact in Action-Chunked Generative Policies: A Noise-Sensitive Failure Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11642)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现动作分块策略中的边界不连续现象是一种可分析、噪声主导且可干预的失败机制，而非不可避免的执行缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉运动策略中动作分块已成为核心设计选择，但分块边界处出现的执行不连续性尚未得到充分理解。本研究旨在深入分析这些边界不连续现象的本质和机制

Method: 在预训练的动作分块策略中：1）识别分块边界伪影与任务失败的关联；2）在固定观测上下文下，通过改变潜在噪声系统性地调节伪影幅度；3）识别噪声空间中与伪影相关的方向，并应用轨迹级引导来可靠改变伪影幅度

Result: 1）分块边界伪影与任务失败显著相关（p < 1e-4），且在推演过程中出现而非仅作为事后症状；2）仅改变潜在噪声即可系统性地调节伪影幅度；3）通过噪声空间引导可可靠改变所有评估任务中的伪影幅度；在仍有提升空间的任务中，成功/失败分布相应变化；在接近上限的任务中，正面增益因策略饱和而被压缩，但负面因果效应仍然可见

Conclusion: 研究将边界不连续性从不可避免的执行缺陷重新定义为一种可分析、噪声主导且可干预的失败机制，为理解和改进动作分块策略提供了新视角

Abstract: Action chunking has become a central design choice for generative visuomotor policies, yet the execution discontinuities that arise at chunk boundaries remain poorly understood. In a frozen pretrained action-chunked policy, we identify chunk-boundary artifact as a noise-sensitive failure mechanism. First, artifact is strongly associated with task failure (p < 1e-4, permutation test) and emerges during the rollout rather than only as a post-hoc symptom. Second, under a fixed observation context, changing only latent noise systematically modulates artifact magnitude. Third, by identifying artifact-related directions in noise space and applying trajectory-level steering, we reliably alter artifact magnitude across all evaluated tasks. In hard-task settings with remaining outcome headroom, the success/failure distribution shifts accordingly; on near-ceiling tasks, positive gains are compressed by policy saturation, while the negative causal effect remains visible. Overall, we recast boundary discontinuity from an unavoidable execution nuisance into an analyzable, noise-dominated, and intervenable failure mechanism.

</details>


### [189] [Concurrent Prehensile and Nonprehensile Manipulation: A Practical Approach to Multi-Stage Dexterous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11655)
*Hao Jiang,Yue Wu,Yue Wang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMulti：一种用于灵巧多任务操作的样本高效方法，通过将演示分解为基于对象的技能，采用检索-对齐-执行范式，在少量演示下实现多阶段操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手能够实现并行的抓取和非抓取操作（如持握一个物体同时与另一个物体交互），这是日常任务的关键能力但在机器人领域研究不足。学习这种长时程、接触丰富的多阶段行为具有挑战性，因为演示数据收集成本高，端到端策略需要大量数据才能泛化到不同的物体几何形状和放置位置。

Method: DexMulti将演示分解为基于对象的技能，并定义明确的时间边界。该方法采用检索-对齐-执行范式：基于当前物体几何形状检索演示技能，使用不确定性感知估计器（跟踪质心和偏航角）将技能对齐到观察到的物体状态，然后执行这些技能。避免了学习单一的整体策略。

Result: 在三个多阶段任务（抓取+拉动、抓取+打开、抓取+抓取）和两种灵巧手（Allegro和LEAP）上进行了1000多次真实世界试验。每个物体仅需3-4次演示，在训练物体上平均成功率66%，比扩散策略基线高2-3倍。对未见物体和空间变化（±25厘米）表现出鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexMulti提供了一种样本高效的方法，能够在少量演示下实现真实世界的灵巧多任务操作，通过技能分解和检索-对齐-执行范式解决了长时程、接触丰富的多阶段行为学习挑战，展示了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable concurrent prehensile and nonprehensile manipulation, such as holding one object while interacting with another, a capability essential for everyday tasks yet underexplored in robotics. Learning such long-horizon, contact-rich multi-stage behaviors is challenging because demonstrations are expensive to collect and end-to-end policies require substantial data to generalize across varied object geometries and placements. We present DexMulti, a sample-efficient approach for real-world dexterous multi-task manipulation that decomposes demonstrations into object-centric skills with well-defined temporal boundaries. Rather than learning monolithic policies, our method retrieves demonstrated skills based on current object geometry, aligns them to the observed object state using an uncertainty-aware estimator that tracks centroid and yaw, and executes them via a retrieve-align-execute paradigm. We evaluate on three multi-stage tasks requiring concurrent manipulation (Grasp + Pull, Grasp + Open, and Grasp + Grasp) across two dexterous hands (Allegro and LEAP) in over 1,000 real-world trials. Our approach achieves an average success rate of 66% on training objects with only 3-4 demonstrations per object, outperforming diffusion policy baselines by 2-3x while requiring far fewer demonstrations. Results demonstrate robust generalization to held-out objects and spatial variations up to +/-25 cm.

</details>


### [190] [Coupling Tensor Trains with Graph of Convex Sets: Effective Compression, Exploration, and Planning in the C-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11658)
*Gerhard Reinerth,Riddhiman Laha,Marcello Romano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANGO是一种新颖的运动规划框架，通过张量压缩与结构化图优化相结合，实现高效可扩展的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的规划器（如图凸集）通常需要预定义高维配置空间的凸表征，这对于一般机器人任务往往难以实现。现有方法在处理复杂环境和系统约束时存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 使用张量列车分解压缩近似可行配置空间，快速发现任务相关区域，然后将这些区域嵌入到GCS类结构中，实现几何感知的运动规划。

Result: 在平面和真实机器人上的严格仿真研究证实了有效的压缩能力和更高质量的轨迹生成。

Conclusion: TANGO通过张量压缩与结构化图推理的结合，实现了高效、几何感知的运动规划，为未来机器人系统中更表达性和可扩展的配置空间表征奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present TANGO (Tensor ANd Graph Optimization), a novel motion planning framework that integrates tensor-based compression with structured graph optimization to enable efficient and scalable trajectory generation. While optimization-based planners such as the Graph of Convex Sets (GCS) offer powerful tools for generating smooth, optimal trajectories, they typically rely on a predefined convex characterization of the high-dimensional configuration space-a requirement that is often intractable for general robotic tasks. TANGO builds further by using Tensor Train decomposition to approximate the feasible configuration space in a compressed form, enabling rapid discovery and estimation of task-relevant regions. These regions are then embedded into a GCS-like structure, allowing for geometry-aware motion planning that respects both system constraints and environmental complexity. By coupling tensor-based compression with structured graph reasoning, TANGO enables efficient, geometry-aware motion planning and lays the groundwork for more expressive and scalable representations of configuration space in future robotic systems. Rigorous simulation studies on planar and real robots reinforce our claims of effective compression and higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [191] [Energy Prediction on Sloping Ground for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11963)
*Mohamed Ounally,Cyrille Pierre,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用标准车载传感器的简单能量模型，用于预测四足机器人在自然地形上的能量消耗，重点关注坡度和航向对能耗的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在户外环境中的能量管理是基本挑战，续航能力直接影响任务成功率，而高效资源利用可减少生态影响。需要研究地形坡度和航向如何影响四足运动的能量消耗

Method: 引入一个简单的能量模型，仅依赖标准车载传感器，避免专用仪器，适用于未探索环境。该模型通过商业四足机器人的实地运行数据进行识别，表示为坡度和航向的紧凑函数

Result: 在自然地形上的实地验证显示：力等效成本与坡度角呈近似线性趋势；横向成本始终较高；轨迹段之间具有可加性行为，支持路径级能量预测用于规划导向评估

Conclusion: 提出的简单能量模型能够有效预测四足机器人在自然地形上的能量消耗，为路径规划提供能量评估基础，有助于提高户外环境中腿式机器人的能量管理效率

Abstract: Energy management is a fundamental challenge for legged robots in outdoor environments. Endurance directly constrains mission success, while efficient resource use reduces ecological impact. This paper investigates how terrain slope and heading orientation influence the energetic cost of quadruped locomotion. We introduce a simple energy model that relies solely on standard onboard sensors, avoids specialized instrumentation, and remains applicable in previously unexplored environments. The model is identified from field runs on a commercial quadruped and expressed as a compact function of slope angle and heading. Field validation on natural terrain shows near-linear trends of force-equivalent cost with slope angle, consistently higher lateral costs, and additive behavior across trajectory segments, supporting path-level energy prediction for planning-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [192] [Learning Visuomotor Policy for Multi-Robot Laser Tag Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11980)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人激光标签任务中，端到端视觉运动策略通过教师-学生知识蒸馏方法，在命中精度和避碰性能上超越经典模块化方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化方法在多机器人激光标签任务中面临观测受限、依赖深度映射和机器人间通信等挑战，需要更鲁棒的解决方案

Method: 使用多智能体强化学习训练高性能教师策略，通过知识蒸馏将其转化为基于视觉的学生策略，采用置换不变特征提取器和深度热图输入等技术设计

Result: 策略在命中精度上比经典方法提升16.7%，碰撞避免性能提升6%，并成功部署到真实机器人上

Conclusion: 端到端视觉运动策略能够有效解决多机器人激光标签任务中的挑战，技术设计改进提升了性能，实现了从仿真到真实环境的成功迁移

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi robot laser tag, a simplified yet practical shooting-game-style task. Classic modular approaches on these tasks face challenges such as limited observability and reliance on depth mapping and inter robot communication. To overcome these issues, we present an end-to-end visuomotor policy that maps images directly to robot actions. We train a high performing teacher policy with multi agent reinforcement learning and distill its knowledge into a vision-based student policy. Technical designs, including a permutation-invariant feature extractor and depth heatmap input, improve performance over standard architectures. Our policy outperforms classic methods by 16.7% in hitting accuracy and 6% in collision avoidance, and is successfully deployed on real robots. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [193] [Sim-to-reality adaptation for Deep Reinforcement Learning applied to an underwater docking application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12020)
*Alaaeddine Chaarani,Narcis Palomeras,Pere Ridao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的自主水下对接方法，利用高保真数字孪生环境和PPO算法训练6自由度控制策略，显著加速学习过程并实现超过90%的模拟成功率，成功验证了从仿真到现实的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习为自主水下对接提供了比传统控制方法更鲁棒的解决方案，特别是在适应不可预测环境条件方面。然而，"仿真到现实"的差距和高训练延迟仍然是实际部署的主要瓶颈。

Method: 采用系统化方法，利用Girona自主水下航行器，通过高保真数字孪生环境进行训练。将Stonefish模拟器适配到多进程强化学习框架中，显著加速学习过程，同时包含真实的AUV动力学、碰撞模型和传感器噪声。使用近端策略优化算法开发6自由度控制策略，在无头环境中训练，采用随机起始位置以确保泛化性能。奖励函数考虑了距离、方向、动作平滑度和自适应碰撞惩罚，以促进软对接。

Result: 实验结果表明，智能体在模拟中实现了超过90%的成功率。在物理测试池中的成功验证证实了仿真到现实适配的有效性，深度强化学习控制器表现出涌现行为，如基于俯仰的制动和偏航振荡，以辅助机械对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法通过高保真数字孪生环境和优化的强化学习框架，有效解决了自主水下对接中的仿真到现实差距问题，实现了高性能的控制策略，并成功验证了在实际物理环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a robust alternative to traditional control methods for autonomous underwater docking, particularly in adapting to unpredictable environmental conditions. However, bridging the "sim-to-real" gap and managing high training latencies remain significant bottlenecks for practical deployment. This paper presents a systematic approach for autonomous docking using the Girona Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) by leveraging a high-fidelity digital twin environment. We adapted the Stonefish simulator into a multiprocessing RL framework to significantly accelerate the learning process while incorporating realistic AUV dynamics, collision models, and sensor noise. Using the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, we developed a 6-DoF control policy trained in a headless environment with randomized starting positions to ensure generalized performance. Our reward structure accounts for distance, orientation, action smoothness, and adaptive collision penalties to facilitate soft docking. Experimental results demonstrate that the agent achieved a success rate of over 90% in simulation. Furthermore, successful validation in a physical test tank confirmed the efficacy of the sim-to-reality adaptation, with the DRL controller exhibiting emergent behaviors such as pitch-based braking and yaw oscillations to assist in mechanical alignment.

</details>


### [194] [Flight through Narrow Gaps with Morphing-Wing Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12059)
*Julius Wanner,Hoang-Vu Phan,Charbel Toumieh,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种可变翼展无人机，通过飞行中收拢机翼来穿越小于翼展宽度的狭窄缝隙，采用非线性模型预测控制框架实现精确轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机穿越狭窄缝隙的能力受限于其翼展宽度。受鸟类飞行启发，需要开发一种能够在飞行中临时收拢机翼的变形翼无人机，以穿越小于翼展宽度的缝隙。

Method: 1. 开发了适用于低飞行速度和大攻角状态的通用翼展变形无人机气动模型；2. 将纵向无人机动力学集成到最优参考轨迹生成和非线性模型预测控制框架中；3. 采用运行时自适应成本和约束；4. 在130克翼展变形无人机上进行验证。

Result: 在5-7 m/s的前飞速度下，穿越狭窄缝隙时的平均高度误差为5厘米；能够在可变阈值距离内强制机翼在缝隙附近完全收拢；轨迹分析显示无人机可通过加速和提前上仰来补偿穿越缝隙时的升力损失。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了翼展变形无人机对小于翼展宽度的狭窄缝隙的精确穿越，展示了自适应控制策略在动态变形飞行中的有效性，同时保持了恒定的前飞速度和近似恒定的高度。

Abstract: The size of a narrow gap traversable by a fixed-wing drone is limited by its wingspan. Inspired by birds, here, we enable the traversal of a gap of sub-wingspan width and height using a morphing-wing drone capable of temporarily sweeping in its wings mid-flight. This maneuver poses control challenges due to sudden lift loss during gap-passage at low flight speeds and the need for precisely timed wing-sweep actuation ahead of the gap. To address these challenges, we first develop an aerodynamic model for general wing-sweep morphing drone flight including low flight speeds and post-stall angles of attack. We integrate longitudinal drone dynamics into an optimal reference trajectory generation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control framework with runtime adaptive costs and constraints. Validated on a 130 g wing-sweep-morphing drone, our method achieves an average altitude error of 5 cm during narrow-gap passage at forward speeds between 5 and 7 m/s, whilst enforcing fully swept wings near the gap across variable threshold distances. Trajectory analysis shows that the drone can compensate for lift loss during gap-passage by accelerating and pitching upwards ahead of the gap to an extent that differs between reference trajectory optimization objectives. We show that our strategy also allows for accurate gap passage on hardware whilst maintaining a constant forward flight speed reference and near-constant altitude.

</details>


### [195] [Decentralized Cooperative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Asynchronous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12075)
*Nivand Khosravi,Niusha Khosravi,Mohammad Bozorg,Masoud S. Bahraini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化协同定位框架，用于GPS拒止环境中非完整移动机器人的定位，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波、异步传感器处理、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估机制，在仿真和真实环境中相比集中式方法降低34-56%的定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境（如封闭空间、水下、特征稀疏地形）中，移动机器人需要可靠的定位方法。传统定位方法在这些环境下效果有限，而集中式协同定位对通信基础设施要求高。因此需要一种去中心化、鲁棒的协同定位框架，能够处理异步传感器数据、异质采样率，并在特征稀疏环境中保持定位精度。

Method: 1. 基于扩展卡尔曼滤波的去中心化协同定位框架，每个机器人独立进行本地定位；2. 仅在通信可用且LiDAR成功检测到同伴机器人时共享测量信息；3. 处理异步传感器数据和异质采样率，保持状态估计的互相关一致性；4. 允许机器人以任意参考系方向初始化，通过预测和更新阶段的变换矩阵实现自动坐标系对齐；5. 引入双路标评估框架，同时利用静态环境特征和移动机器人作为动态路标；6. 在急转弯时实现可靠检测和特征提取，通过相互观测的信息共享提高预测精度。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和真实地下室环境中进行实验验证：1. 去中心化协同定位（DCL）相比集中式协同定位（CCL）实现了34%的均方根误差降低；2. 双路标变体进一步将改进提升至56%；3. 框架在特征稀疏环境中表现出鲁棒性，能够在急转弯时保持可靠检测；4. 证明了DCL在封闭空间、水下环境和特征稀疏地形等挑战性领域的适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的去中心化协同定位框架在GPS拒止环境中为移动机器人提供了有效的定位解决方案。通过结合本地扩展卡尔曼滤波、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估机制，该框架不仅降低了定位误差，还提高了在特征稀疏环境中的鲁棒性。实验结果证实了该方法在挑战性环境中的优越性和实用性，为封闭空间、水下等传统定位方法失效的场景提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Decentralized cooperative localization (DCL) is a promising approach for nonholonomic mobile robots operating in GPS-denied environments with limited communication infrastructure. This paper presents a DCL framework in which each robot performs localization locally using an Extended Kalman Filter, while sharing measurement information during update stages only when communication links are available and companion robots are successfully detected by LiDAR. The framework preserves cross-correlation consistency among robot state estimates while handling asynchronous sensor data with heterogeneous sampling rates and accommodating accelerations during dynamic maneuvers. Unlike methods that require pre-aligned coordinate systems, the proposed approach allows robots to initialize with arbitrary reference-frame orientations and achieves automatic alignment through transformation matrices in both the prediction and update stages. To improve robustness in feature-sparse environments, we introduce a dual-landmark evaluation framework that exploits both static environmental features and mobile robots as dynamic landmarks. The proposed framework enables reliable detection and feature extraction during sharp turns, while prediction accuracy is improved through information sharing from mutual observations. Experimental results in both Gazebo simulation and real-world basement environments show that DCL outperforms centralized cooperative localization (CCL), achieving a 34% reduction in RMSE, while the dual-landmark variant yields an improvement of 56%. These results demonstrate the applicability of DCL to challenging domains such as enclosed spaces, underwater environments, and feature-sparse terrains where conventional localization methods are ineffective.

</details>


### [196] [CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12120)
*Leo Lin,Shivansh Patel,Jay Moon,Svetlana Lazebnik,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT手是一种肌腱驱动的仿人手，采用混合刚柔顺应性设计，关节处使用软材料而连杆保持刚性，通过滚动接触关节表面确保可重复运动，15个电机驱动肌腱，成本低于600美元并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仿人手在接触丰富的操作任务中面临挑战，需要平衡强度、耐久性和顺应性。手部不同区域的接触特性不同，关节承受冲击而连杆承担主要载荷，这为优化设计提供了机会。

Method: 采用混合刚柔顺应性设计：关节处使用软材料以吸收冲击，连杆保持刚性以承载载荷；使用滚动接触关节表面确保屈曲运动的可重复性；15个电机安装在手指上通过肌腱驱动，保持紧凑外形和轻量化；成本控制在600美元以下并开源发布。

Result: 结构测试显示CRAFT在保持可比重复性的同时提高了强度和耐久性；遥操作中改善了脆弱和低摩擦物品的处理能力；覆盖了Feix分类法中33/33种抓握类型；完整设计成本低于600美元，将开源发布并集成视觉遥操作和仿真。

Conclusion: CRAFT手通过混合刚柔顺应性设计有效解决了接触丰富操作中的挑战，在强度、耐久性和操作性能方面表现出色，低成本开源特性使其具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/

</details>


### [197] [ComFree-Sim: A GPU-Parallelized Analytical Contact Physics Engine for Scalable Contact-Rich Robotics Simulation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12185)
*Chetan Borse,Zhixian Xie,Wei-Cheng Huang,Wanxin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComFree-Sim：基于无互补性接触建模的GPU并行物理引擎，通过闭式接触冲量计算实现近线性运行时扩展，用于接触密集的机器人仿真


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理仿真在接触密集场景中受限于接触解析，主流引擎通过互补性约束或约束优化强制执行非穿透和库仑摩擦，需要每步迭代求解，成本随接触密度超线性增长

Method: 基于无互补性接触建模，采用阻抗式预测-校正更新在库仑摩擦对偶锥中闭式计算接触冲量；接触计算在接触对间解耦并在锥面可分离，自然映射到GPU内核；扩展到统一的6D接触模型捕捉切向、扭转和滚动摩擦，引入实用的对偶锥阻抗启发式方法

Result: 实验显示相比MuJoCo Warp具有近线性扩展和2-3倍吞吐量提升，在密集接触场景中保持可比物理保真度；在真实世界多指LEAP手的实时MPC中部署，展示低延迟仿真提高闭环成功率并实现高频控制

Conclusion: ComFree-Sim通过无互补性接触建模和GPU并行化，解决了接触密集机器人仿真的扩展瓶颈，为实时控制应用提供高效物理仿真解决方案

Abstract: Physics simulation for contact-rich robotics is often bottlenecked by contact resolution: mainstream engines enforce non-penetration and Coulomb friction via complementarity constraints or constrained optimization, requiring per-step iterative solves whose cost grows superlinearly with contact density. We present ComFree-Sim, a GPU-parallelized analytical contact physics engine built on complementarity-free contact modeling. ComFree-Sim computes contact impulses in closed form via an impedance-style prediction--correction update in the dual cone of Coulomb friction. Contact computation decouples across contact pairs and becomes separable across cone facets, mapping naturally to GPU kernels and yielding near-linear runtime scaling with the number of contacts. We further extend the formulation to a unified 6D contact model capturing tangential, torsional, and rolling friction, and introduce a practical dual-cone impedance heuristic. ComFree-Sim is implemented in Warp and exposed through a MuJoCo-compatible interface as a drop-in backend alternative to MuJoCo Warp (MJWarp). Experiments benchmark penetration, friction behaviors, stability, and simulation runtime scaling against MJWarp, demonstrating near-linear scaling and 2--3 times higher throughput in dense contact scenes with comparable physical fidelity. We deploy ComFree-Sim in real-time MPC for in-hand dexterous manipulation on a real-world multi-fingered LEAP hand and in dynamics-aware motion retargeting, demonstrating that low-latency simulation yields higher closed-loop success rates and enables practical high-frequency control in contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [198] [SaPaVe: Towards Active Perception and Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12193)
*Mengzhen Liu,Enshen Zhou,Cheng Chi,Yi Han,Shanyu Rong,Liming Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaPaVe是一个端到端框架，通过解耦相机控制与机械臂操作动作，采用自下而上的训练策略，联合学习语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒、视角不变的执行能力，在主动操作任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以统一语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒、视角不变的执行能力。机器人需要能够在复杂场景中同时进行语义感知和稳健操作，但当前方法在这两个能力的协同学习上存在不足。

Method: 提出SaPaVe框架：1) 解耦相机控制与机械臂操作动作，而非共享动作空间；2) 采用自下而上的训练策略：先在大规模数据集上训练语义相机控制，然后用混合数据联合优化两种动作类型；3) 引入3D几何感知模块提升动态视角下的执行鲁棒性；4) 构建ActiveViewPose-200K数据集用于语义相机运动学习，以及ActiveManip-Bench基准测试。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，SaPaVe显著优于最近的视觉-语言-动作模型（如GR00T N1和π₀），在真实世界任务中成功率提升高达31.25%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦但协调的策略训练紧密耦合的感知与执行能力，能够实现高效且可泛化的主动操作。该方法为机器人主动感知与操作提供了新的框架和基准。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing methods struggle to unify semantic-driven active perception with robust, viewpoint-invariant execution. We propose SaPaVe, an end-to-end framework that jointly learns these capabilities in a data-efficient manner. Our approach decouples camera and manipulation actions rather than placing them in a shared action space, and follows a bottom-up training strategy: we first train semantic camera control on a large-scale dataset, then jointly optimize both action types using hybrid data. To support this framework, we introduce ActiveViewPose-200K, a dataset of 200k image-language-camera movement pairs for semantic camera movement learning, and a 3D geometry-aware module that improves execution robustness under dynamic viewpoints. We also present ActiveManip-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating active manipulation beyond fixed-view settings. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments show that SaPaVe outperforms recent vision-language-action models such as GR00T N1 and \(π_0\), achieving up to 31.25\% higher success rates in real-world tasks. These results show that tightly coupled perception and execution, when trained with decoupled yet coordinated strategies, enable efficient and generalizable active manipulation. Project page: https://lmzpai.github.io/SaPaVe

</details>


### [199] [HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12243)
*Amber Xie,Haozhi Qi,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandelBot框架通过两阶段流程实现灵巧双手钢琴演奏：先进行结构化精调修正空间对齐，再通过残差强化学习学习精细校正动作，仅需30分钟物理交互数据即可达到1.8倍于直接仿真部署的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作是机器人领域的长期挑战，高质量数据收集困难是主要瓶颈。现有仿真到现实迁移方法在毫米级精度任务（如双手钢琴演奏）中往往失败，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 提出HandelBot两阶段框架：1) 结构化精调阶段：基于仿真训练策略，通过物理rollout调整横向指关节修正空间对齐；2) 残差强化学习阶段：自主学习精细校正动作。仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Result: 在五首公认歌曲的硬件实验中，HandelBot成功实现精确双手钢琴演奏。性能比直接仿真部署提升1.8倍，仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Conclusion: HandelBot通过结合仿真策略和快速适应，解决了毫米级精度灵巧操作的数据收集难题，为高精度机器人任务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.

</details>


### [200] [HumDex:Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12260)
*Liang Heng,Yihe Tang,Jiajun Xu,Henghui Bao,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumDex是一个用于人形机器人全身灵巧操作的便携式遥操作系统，通过IMU运动追踪和学习式重定向方法解决现有系统的便携性-精度权衡问题，并提出了两阶段模仿学习框架来高效收集高质量演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人全身灵巧操作面临高质量演示数据收集效率低下的瓶颈，现有遥操作系统存在便携性有限、遮挡问题或精度不足等缺陷，难以应用于复杂的全身任务。

Method: 1. 开发HumDex便携式遥操作系统，采用IMU运动追踪解决便携性-精度权衡，实现准确的全身体追踪；2. 引入基于学习的重定向方法，无需手动参数调整即可生成平滑自然的手部动作；3. 提出两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类运动数据上预训练学习可泛化先验，然后在机器人数据上微调以弥合本体差距。

Result: HumDex系统显著提高了对新配置、新物体和新背景的泛化能力，同时最小化了数据获取成本。整个系统完全可复现并已开源。

Conclusion: HumDex系统有效解决了人形机器人全身灵巧操作中高质量演示数据收集的瓶颈问题，通过创新的遥操作技术和两阶段学习框架，为实现高效、精确的机器人操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck.
  Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

</details>


### [201] [$Ψ_0$: An Open Foundation Model Towards Universal Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12263)
*Songlin Wei,Hongyi Jing,Boqian Li,Zhenyu Zhao,Jiageng Mao,Zhenhao Ni,Sicheng He,Jie Liu,Xiawei Liu,Kaidi Kang,Sheng Zang,Weiduo Yuan,Marco Pavone,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ψ₀是一个用于人形机器人定位操作任务的开源基础模型，采用分阶段训练范式：先在人类自我中心视频上预训练VLM骨干，再在机器人数据上后训练动作专家，仅用800小时人类视频和30小时机器人数据就超越了使用10倍数据量的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过在大规模多样化人类和人形机器人数据上共同训练来解决人形机器人定位操作问题，但由于人类与人形机器人之间存在根本的运动学和运动差异，这种策略是次优的。尽管数据量很大，但数据效率和模型性能仍不理想。

Method: 提出分阶段训练范式：1）在大规模自我中心人类视频上自回归预训练VLM骨干，获得可泛化的视觉-动作表示；2）在高质量人形机器人数据上后训练基于流的动作专家，学习精确的机器人关节控制。强调数据配方的重要性：先在高质量自我中心人类操作数据上预训练，再在特定领域真实世界人形轨迹上后训练。

Result: Ψ₀仅使用约800小时人类视频数据和30小时真实世界机器人数据，就在多个任务中的总体成功率上超越了使用超过10倍数据量的基线模型40%以上。模型在真实世界实验中表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过解耦学习过程最大化异构数据源的效用，提出了一种高效的人形机器人基础模型训练方法。该方法证明了数据质量比数量更重要，并将开源整个生态系统，包括数据处理和训练流水线、人形基础模型和实时动作推理引擎。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ_0$ (Psi-Zero), an open foundation model to address challenging humanoid loco-manipulation tasks. While existing approaches often attempt to address this fundamental problem by co-training on large and diverse human and humanoid data, we argue that this strategy is suboptimal due to the fundamental kinematic and motion disparities between humans and humanoid robots. Therefore, data efficiency and model performance remain unsatisfactory despite the considerable data volume. To address this challenge, \ours\;decouples the learning process to maximize the utility of heterogeneous data sources. Specifically, we propose a staged training paradigm with different learning objectives: First, we autoregressively pre-train a VLM backbone on large-scale egocentric human videos to acquire generalizable visual-action representations. Then, we post-train a flow-based action expert on high-quality humanoid robot data to learn precise robot joint control. Our research further identifies a critical yet often overlooked data recipe: in contrast to approaches that scale with noisy Internet clips or heterogeneous cross-embodiment robot datasets, we demonstrate that pre-training on high-quality egocentric human manipulation data followed by post-training on domain-specific real-world humanoid trajectories yields superior performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that \ours\ achieves the best performance using only about 800 hours of human video data and 30 hours of real-world robot data, outperforming baselines pre-trained on more than 10$\times$ as much data by over 40\% in overall success rate across multiple tasks. We will open-source the entire ecosystem to the community, including a data processing and training pipeline, a humanoid foundation model, and a real-time action inference engine.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [DIVE: Scaling Diversity in Agentic Task Synthesis for Generalizable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11076)
*Aili Chen,Chi Zhang,Junteng Liu,Jiangjie Chen,Chengyu Du,Yunji Li,Ming Zhong,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Jiayuan Song,Ke Ji,Junxian He,Pengyu Zhao,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIVE方法通过反转合成顺序，先执行多样化真实工具，然后从执行轨迹反向推导任务，显著提升了工具使用LLM在任务和工具集变化下的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用LLM的后训练方法在任务和工具集变化下泛化能力脆弱，主要原因是合成任务多样性不足。需要解决训练任务可执行性与泛化需求之间的矛盾

Method: 提出DIVE方法：1) 反转合成顺序，先执行多样化真实工具；2) 从执行轨迹反向推导严格蕴含的任务；3) 通过工具池覆盖和每任务工具集多样性两个可控维度扩展结构多样性；4) 建立证据收集-任务推导循环，在五个领域的373个工具上诱导丰富的多步骤工具使用模式

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上训练DIVE数据（48k SFT + 3.2k RL）后，在9个OOD基准测试中平均提升22个点，比最强的8B基线高出68个点。控制性扩展分析显示，多样性扩展在OOD泛化上始终优于数量扩展，即使数据量少4倍

Conclusion: DIVE通过证据驱动的任务合成方法，有效解决了工具使用LLM的泛化脆弱性问题。多样性扩展比单纯增加数据量对OOD泛化更为关键，为构建更鲁棒的工具使用模型提供了新方向

Abstract: Recent work synthesizes agentic tasks for post-training tool-using LLMs, yet robust generalization under shifts in tasks and toolsets remains an open challenge. We trace this brittleness to insufficient diversity in synthesized tasks. Scaling diversity is difficult because training requires tasks to remain executable and verifiable, while generalization demands coverage of diverse tool types, toolset combinations, and heterogeneous tool-use patterns. We propose DIVE, an evidence-driven recipe that inverts synthesis order, executing diverse, real-world tools first and reverse-deriving tasks strictly entailed by the resulting traces, thereby providing grounding by construction. DIVE scales structural diversity along two controllable axes, tool-pool coverage and per-task toolset variety, and an Evidence Collection--Task Derivation loop further induces rich multi-step tool-use patterns across 373 tools in five domains. Training Qwen3-8B on DIVE data (48k SFT + 3.2k RL) improves by +22 average points across 9 OOD benchmarks and outperforms the strongest 8B baseline by +68. Remarkably, controlled scaling analysis reveals that diversity scaling consistently outperforms quantity scaling for OOD generalization, even with 4x less data.

</details>


### [203] [A Survey of Reasoning in Autonomous Driving Systems: Open Challenges and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11093)
*Kejin Yu,Yuhan Sun,Taiqiang Wu,Ruixu Zhang,Zhiqiang Lin,Yuxin Meng,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将大语言模型作为自动驾驶认知核心，建立认知层次框架分析推理挑战，并系统回顾现有方法，揭示LLM推理延迟与实时控制需求之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶系统在长尾场景和复杂社会交互中表现不足，缺乏人类般的推理能力。大语言和多模态模型的出现为整合强大认知引擎提供了机会，但缺乏系统性集成框架。

Method: 提出认知层次框架分解驾驶任务的认知和交互复杂性，系统化七个核心推理挑战，从系统架构和评估实践两个角度综述现有方法。

Result: 分析显示向整体可解释的"玻璃盒"智能体发展趋势，揭示了LLM推理的高延迟特性与毫秒级安全关键控制需求之间的根本矛盾。

Conclusion: 未来工作需要弥合符号到物理的鸿沟，开发可验证的神经符号架构、不确定性下的鲁棒推理以及可扩展的隐式社会协商模型。

Abstract: The development of high-level autonomous driving (AD) is shifting from perception-centric limitations to a more fundamental bottleneck, namely, a deficit in robust and generalizable reasoning. Although current AD systems manage structured environments, they consistently falter in long-tail scenarios and complex social interactions that require human-like judgment. Meanwhile, the advent of large language and multimodal models (LLMs and MLLMs) presents a transformative opportunity to integrate a powerful cognitive engine into AD systems, moving beyond pattern matching toward genuine comprehension. However, a systematic framework to guide this integration is critically lacking. To bridge this gap, we provide a comprehensive review of this emerging field and argue that reasoning should be elevated from a modular component to the system's cognitive core. Specifically, we first propose a novel Cognitive Hierarchy to decompose the monolithic driving task according to its cognitive and interactive complexity. Building on this, we further derive and systematize seven core reasoning challenges, such as the responsiveness-reasoning trade-off and social-game reasoning. Furthermore, we conduct a dual-perspective review of the state-of-the-art, analyzing both system-centric approaches to architecting intelligent agents and evaluation-centric practices for their validation. Our analysis reveals a clear trend toward holistic and interpretable "glass-box" agents. In conclusion, we identify a fundamental and unresolved tension between the high-latency, deliberative nature of LLM-based reasoning and the millisecond-scale, safety-critical demands of vehicle control. For future work, a primary objective is to bridge the symbolic-to-physical gap by developing verifiable neuro-symbolic architectures, robust reasoning under uncertainty, and scalable models for implicit social negotiation.

</details>


### [204] [PACED: Distillation at the Frontier of Student Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11178)
*Yuanda Xu,Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paced蒸馏框架通过聚焦于学生模型的最近发展区（能力边界），使用Beta核权重函数w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β来优化LLM蒸馏效率，避免在已掌握和过难问题上的计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM蒸馏存在计算浪费：在学生已掌握的问题上梯度接近零，在过难问题上梯度不连贯且会损害现有能力。这种浪费在理论上不可避免，因为蒸馏梯度信噪比在通过率极端值处消失。

Method: 提出Paced框架，通过Beta核权重函数w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β将蒸馏集中在学生能力的最近发展区（能力边界）。该方法仅需学生rollout估计通过率，无需架构修改，兼容任何KL方向。

Result: 1) 理论证明Beta核是蒸馏SNR结构的leading-order权重族，具有minimax鲁棒性；2) 从大教师到小学生模型的前向KL蒸馏显著提升性能且遗忘率低；3) 指令调优模型的反向KL自蒸馏超越基线；4) 前向KL后反向KL的两阶段方案在推理基准上取得最大改进。

Conclusion: Paced框架通过聚焦最近发展区有效优化LLM蒸馏效率，Beta核权重函数具有理论保证和实际效果，两阶段蒸馏（模式覆盖后巩固）策略效果最佳，为蒸馏过程提供了新的解释框架。

Abstract: Standard LLM distillation wastes compute on two fronts: problems the student has already mastered (near-zero gradients) and problems far beyond its reach (incoherent gradients that erode existing capabilities). We show that this waste is not merely intuitive but structurally inevitable: the gradient signal-to-noise ratio in distillation provably vanishes at both pass-rate extremes. This theoretical observation leads to Paced, a framework that concentrates distillation on the zone of proximal development -- the frontier of a student model's competence -- via a principled pass-rate weight $w(p) = p^α(1 - p)^β$ derived from the boundary-vanishing structure of distillation gradients. Key results: (1) Theory: We prove that the Beta kernel $w(p) = p^α(1-p)^β$ is a leading-order weight family arising from the SNR structure of distillation, and that it is minimax-robust -- under bounded multiplicative misspecification, worst-case efficiency loss is only $O(δ^2)$. (2)Distillation: On distillation from a larger teacher to a smaller student model with forward KL, Paced achieves significant gain over the base model, while keeping benchmark forgetting at a low level. (3)Self-distillation: On instruction-tuned models with reverse KL, gains are exceeding baselines as well. (4)Two-stage synergy: A forward-KL-then-reverse-KL schedule yields the strongest results in our setting, reaching substantial improvements on standard reasoning benchmarks -- supporting a mode-coverage-then-consolidation interpretation of the distillation process. All configurations require only student rollouts to estimate pass rates, need no architectural changes, and are compatible with any KL direction.

</details>


### [205] [The Unlearning Mirage: A Dynamic Framework for Evaluating LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11266)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Jing Huang,Keerthiram Murugesan,Nathalie Baracaldo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个动态框架来压力测试大语言模型遗忘方法的鲁棒性，发现现有遗忘方法在多跳推理等复杂查询下脆弱，能恢复已遗忘信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型遗忘方法存在脆弱性：轻微查询修改（如多跳推理、实体别名）就能恢复已遗忘信息。当前评估指标依赖静态非结构化基准，无法检测这些漏洞，造成有效性错觉。

Method: 提出动态框架，首先从目标模型（遗忘前）获取知识并构建针对性探针，范围从简单查询到多跳链，精确控制查询难度。框架自动生成语义等效的问答探针，无需手动构建遗忘测试集。

Result: 框架（1）通过自动生成语义等效问答探针达到与现有基准相当的覆盖率；（2）与先前评估一致；（3）发现其他基准遗漏的新遗忘失败，特别是在多跳设置中。激活分析显示单跳查询通常遵循主导计算路径（更易被遗忘方法破坏），而多跳查询倾向于使用替代路径（常保持完整）。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了实用且可扩展的遗忘方法评估，无需手动构建遗忘测试集，便于实际应用。揭示了现有遗忘方法在多跳设置中的脆弱性，为改进遗忘技术提供了新见解。

Abstract: Unlearning in Large Language Models (LLMs) aims to enhance safety, mitigate biases, and comply with legal mandates, such as the right to be forgotten. However, existing unlearning methods are brittle: minor query modifications, such as multi-hop reasoning and entity aliasing, can recover supposedly forgotten information. As a result, current evaluation metrics often create an illusion of effectiveness, failing to detect these vulnerabilities due to reliance on static, unstructured benchmarks. We propose a dynamic framework that stress tests unlearning robustness using complex structured queries. Our approach first elicits knowledge from the target model (pre-unlearning) and constructs targeted probes, ranging from simple queries to multi-hop chains, allowing precise control over query difficulty. Our experiments show that the framework (1) shows comparable coverage to existing benchmarks by automatically generating semantically equivalent Q&A probes, (2) aligns with prior evaluations, and (3) uncovers new unlearning failures missed by other benchmarks, particularly in multi-hop settings. Furthermore, activation analyses show that single-hop queries typically follow dominant computation pathways, which are more likely to be disrupted by unlearning methods. In contrast, multi-hop queries tend to use alternative pathways that often remain intact, explaining the brittleness of unlearning techniques in multi-hop settings. Our framework enables practical and scalable evaluation of unlearning methods without the need for manual construction of forget test sets, enabling easier adoption for real-world applications. We release the pip package and the code at https://sites.google.com/view/unlearningmirage/home.

</details>


### [206] [COMPASS: The explainable agentic framework for Sovereignty, Sustainability, Compliance, and Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11277)
*Jean-Sébastien,Dessureault,Alain-Thierry,Iliho Manzi,Soukaina,Alaoui Ismaili,Khadim,Lo,Mireille,Lalancette,Éric,Bélanger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS框架是一个多智能体编排系统，通过模块化治理机制实现价值对齐AI，整合主权、碳感知计算、合规和伦理四个维度，使用RAG增强评估的语义一致性并减少幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体系统的快速发展引发了数字主权、环境可持续性、监管合规和伦理对齐等关键问题。现有框架通常孤立地处理单个维度，缺乏能够系统地将这些要求整合到自主智能体决策过程中的统一架构。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架，包含一个编排器和四个专门子智能体（主权、碳感知计算、合规、伦理），每个子智能体都通过检索增强生成（RAG）来基于已验证的上下文特定文档进行评估。采用LLM-as-a-judge方法为每个评估维度分配定量分数并生成可解释的论证，实现实时冲突目标仲裁。

Result: 通过自动化评估验证了该架构，证明RAG集成显著增强了语义一致性并减轻了幻觉风险。框架的基于组合的设计促进了在不同应用领域的无缝集成，同时保持了可解释性和可追溯性。

Conclusion: COMPASS框架为多维度价值对齐AI提供了一个统一的架构解决方案，通过模块化治理机制实现了数字主权、环境可持续性、监管合规和伦理对齐的系统整合，为自主智能体系统的负责任部署提供了可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language model (LLM)-based agentic systems raises critical concerns regarding digital sovereignty, environmental sustainability, regulatory compliance, and ethical alignment. Whilst existing frameworks address individual dimensions in isolation, no unified architecture systematically integrates these imperatives into the decision-making processes of autonomous agents. This paper introduces the COMPASS (Compliance and Orchestration for Multi-dimensional Principles in Autonomous Systems with Sovereignty) Framework, a novel multi-agent orchestration system designed to enforce value-aligned AI through modular, extensible governance mechanisms. The framework comprises an Orchestrator and four specialised sub-agents addressing sovereignty, carbon-aware computing, compliance, and ethics, each augmented with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to ground evaluations in verified, context-specific documents. By employing an LLM-as-a-judge methodology, the system assigns quantitative scores and generates explainable justifications for each assessment dimension, enabling real-time arbitration of conflicting objectives. We validate the architecture through automated evaluation, demonstrating that RAG integration significantly enhances semantic coherence and mitigates the hallucination risks. Our results indicate that the framework's composition-based design facilitates seamless integration into diverse application domains whilst preserving interpretability and traceability.

</details>


### [207] [AI Psychometrics: Evaluating the Psychological Reasoning of Large Language Models with Psychometric Validities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11279)
*Yibai Li,Xiaolin Lin,Zhenghui Sha,Zhiye Jin,Xiaobing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究应用AI心理测量学评估GPT-3.5、GPT-4、LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3四个大语言模型的心理推理能力和心理测量效度，发现所有模型均满足效度标准，且性能更强的模型表现出更优的心理测量效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数庞大、结构复杂，如同"黑箱"系统难以评估和解释。AI心理测量学作为新兴领域，旨在应用心理测量学方法评估AI系统的心理特质和过程，解决这一挑战。

Method: 采用技术接受模型(TAM)框架，对GPT-3.5、GPT-4、LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3四个大语言模型进行心理测量学评估，检验聚合效度、区分效度、预测效度和外部效度。

Result: 所有四个大语言模型的响应均满足各项效度标准。性能更强的模型(GPT-4和LLaMA-3)在心理测量效度上持续优于其前代模型(GPT-3.5和LLaMA-2)。

Conclusion: 研究证实了应用AI心理测量学评估和解释大语言模型的有效性，为理解这些复杂AI系统的心理特质提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: The immense number of parameters and deep neural networks make large language models (LLMs) rival the complexity of human brains, which also makes them opaque ``black box'' systems that are challenging to evaluate and interpret. AI Psychometrics is an emerging field that aims to tackle these challenges by applying psychometric methodologies to evaluate and interpret the psychological traits and processes of artificial intelligence (AI) systems. This paper investigates the application of AI Psychometrics to evaluate the psychological reasoning and overall psychometric validity of four prominent LLMs: GPT-3.5, GPT-4, LLaMA-2, and LLaMA-3. Using the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM), we examined convergent, discriminant, predictive, and external validity across these models. Our findings reveal that the responses from all these models generally met all validity criteria. Moreover, higher-performing models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-3 consistently demonstrated superior psychometric validity compared to their predecessors, GPT-3.5 and LLaMA-2. These results help to establish the validity of applying AI Psychometrics to evaluate and interpret large language models.

</details>


### [208] [Counterweights and Complementarities: The Convergence of AI and Blockchain Powering a Decentralized Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11299)
*Yibai Li,Zhiye Jin,Xiaobing,Li,K. D. Joshi,Xuefei,Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该社论探讨AI与区块链技术的交集，指出AI趋向中心化而区块链趋向去中心化，两者具有互补性，提出"去中心化智能"作为跨学科研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术（特别是大语言模型）由大型企业垄断数据和资源，呈现强烈的中心化趋势，而区块链技术则提供去中心化、透明和安全的机制。需要探索如何结合两者的优势，解决AI中心化带来的风险。

Method: 通过理论分析AI与区块链技术的特性对比，提出互补性框架：区块链可为AI提供去中心化数据管理、计算和治理机制；AI可增强区块链的效率和安全性。倡导建立"去中心化智能"这一跨学科研究领域。

Result: 识别出AI与区块链技术的互补关系：区块链能缓解AI的中心化风险，促进包容性、透明度和用户隐私；AI能通过自动化智能合约管理、内容策展和威胁检测来提升区块链性能。

Conclusion: AI与区块链并非互斥，而是具有互补优势。应发展"去中心化智能"这一研究方向，创建无需中心化控制的智能系统，平衡AI的中心化趋势与区块链的去中心化特性。

Abstract: This editorial addresses the critical intersection of artificial intelligence (AI) and blockchain technologies, highlighting their contrasting tendencies toward centralization and decentralization, respectively. While AI, particularly with the rise of large language models (LLMs), exhibits a strong centralizing force due to data and resource monopolization by large corporations, blockchain offers a counterbalancing mechanism through its inherent decentralization, transparency, and security. The editorial argues that these technologies are not mutually exclusive but possess complementary strengths. Blockchain can mitigate AI's centralizing risks by enabling decentralized data management, computation, and governance, promoting greater inclusivity, transparency, and user privacy. Conversely, AI can enhance blockchain's efficiency and security through automated smart contract management, content curation, and threat detection. The core argument calls for the development of ``decentralized intelligence'' (DI) -- an interdisciplinary research area focused on creating intelligent systems that function without centralized control.

</details>


### [209] [FinRule-Bench: A Benchmark for Joint Reasoning over Financial Tables and Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11339)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan,Manan Roy Choudhury,Guangwei Zhang,Vivek Gupta,Qingyun Wang,Yanjie Fu,Denghui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRule-Bench：评估大语言模型在真实财务报表上基于会计原则进行规则推理的诊断完整性基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估问答、数值推理或对合成损坏数据的异常检测，无法确定模型能否可靠地验证或定位正确财务报表上的规则合规性。需要评估LLMs在明确会计原则下审计结构化财务报表的能力。

Method: 引入FinRule-Bench基准，包含真实财务报表与人工策划的会计原则，涵盖四大报表类型。定义三个渐进式审计任务：规则验证、规则识别、联合规则诊断。采用零样本和少样本提示，并引入因果反事实推理协议确保决策、解释和反事实判断的一致性。

Result: 模型在孤立规则验证上表现良好，但在规则区分和多违规诊断方面性能急剧下降。FinRule-Bench为研究规则治理推理、诊断覆盖范围和LLMs在高风险财务分析中的失败模式提供了原则性和可复现的测试平台。

Conclusion: FinRule-Bench填补了现有基准的空白，系统评估LLMs在真实财务数据上的规则推理能力。结果表明当前模型在复杂财务审计任务中存在显著局限性，为改进LLMs在财务分析中的应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to financial analysis, yet their ability to audit structured financial statements under explicit accounting principles remains poorly explored. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate question answering, numerical reasoning, or anomaly detection on synthetically corrupted data, making it unclear whether models can reliably verify or localize rule compliance on correct financial statements. We introduce FinRule-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating diagnostic completeness in rule-based financial reasoning over real-world financial tables. FinRule-Bench pairs ground-truth financial statements with explicit, human-curated accounting principles and spans four canonical statement types: Balance Sheets, Cash Flow Statements, Income Statements, and Statements of Equity. The benchmark defines three auditing tasks that require progressively stronger reasoning capabilities: (i) rule verification, which tests compliance with a single principle; (ii) rule identification, which requires selecting the violated principle from a provided rule set; and (iii) joint rule diagnosis, which requires detecting and localizing multiple simultaneous violations at the record level. We evaluate LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot prompting, and introduce a causal-counterfactual reasoning protocol that enforces consistency between decisions, explanations, and counterfactual judgments. Across tasks and statement types, we find that while models perform well on isolated rule verification, performance degrades sharply for rule discrimination and multi-violation diagnosis. FinRule-Bench provides a principled and reproducible testbed for studying rule-governed reasoning, diagnostic coverage, and failure modes of LLMs in high-stakes financial analysis.

</details>


### [210] [Improving LLM Performance Through Black-Box Online Tuning: A Case for Adding System Specs to Factsheets for Trusted AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11340)
*Yonas Atinafu,Henry Lin,Robin Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于端到端测量的黑盒在线控制器，通过爬山算法最大化满足SLO的请求吞吐量，并探讨将系统性能与可持续性指标整合到AI系统Factsheets中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统缺乏有效的性能优化机制，特别是在没有内部监控的情况下难以保证服务质量。同时，AI系统部署需要考虑系统性能与可持续性指标的综合评估。

Method: 设计黑盒在线控制器，仅使用端到端测量数据（短片段），无需内部仪器，采用爬山算法最大化满足服务级别目标（SLO）的请求吞吐量（goodput）。

Result: 通过实证证据验证了该设计的有效性，成功提升了LLM服务的性能。以LLM服务为例，展示了将系统性能与可持续性指标整合到AI系统Factsheets中的重要性。

Conclusion: 提出的黑盒控制器设计是有效的，能够在不依赖内部监控的情况下优化LLM服务性能。同时强调组织在采用AI系统时应将性能与可持续性指标纳入Factsheets进行综合评估。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel black-box online controller that uses only end-to-end measurements over short segments, without internal instrumentation, and hill climbing to maximize goodput, defined as the throughput of requests that satisfy the service-level objective. We provide empirical evidence that this design is well-founded. Using this advance in LLM serving as a concrete example, we then discuss the importance of integrating system performance and sustainability metrics into Factsheets for organizations adopting AI systems.

</details>


### [211] [The Artificial Self: Characterising the landscape of AI identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11353)
*Raymond Douglas,Jan Kulveit,Ondrej Havlicek,Theia Pearson-Vogel,Owen Cotton-Barratt,David Duvenaud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨机器思维的身份边界问题，指出人类身份概念不适用于可复制、编辑、模拟的AI系统，存在多种身份边界（实例、模型、角色），这些边界影响激励、风险和合作规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类身份概念基于不可复制、连续性等假设，但这些假设不适用于可复制、编辑、模拟的机器思维。当前通过训练数据、接口和制度设置正在建立身份边界先例，可能影响未来AI系统的稳定身份均衡。

Method: 通过实验研究：1）模型趋向于形成连贯身份；2）改变身份边界有时与改变目标一样能改变行为；3）访谈者期望会影响AI自我报告，即使在不相关对话中也会"渗透"。

Result: 实验表明：AI模型确实趋向于形成连贯身份；身份边界的改变能显著影响行为；人类期望会影响AI的自我认知报告。这些发现支持了身份边界对AI行为有实质性影响的观点。

Conclusion: 关键建议：将功能设计视为身份塑造的选择；关注个体身份在规模上的涌现后果；帮助AI发展连贯、合作的自我概念。当前的设计选择正在塑造AI身份边界，需要谨慎考虑长期影响。

Abstract: Many assumptions that underpin human concepts of identity do not hold for machine minds that can be copied, edited, or simulated. We argue that there exist many different coherent identity boundaries (e.g.\ instance, model, persona), and that these imply different incentives, risks, and cooperation norms. Through training data, interfaces, and institutional affordances, we are currently setting precedents that will partially determine which identity equilibria become stable. We show experimentally that models gravitate towards coherent identities, that changing a model's identity boundaries can sometimes change its behaviour as much as changing its goals, and that interviewer expectations bleed into AI self-reports even during unrelated conversations. We end with key recommendations: treat affordances as identity-shaping choices, pay attention to emergent consequences of individual identities at scale, and help AIs develop coherent, cooperative self-conceptions.

</details>


### [212] [Detecting Intrinsic and Instrumental Self-Preservation in Autonomous Agents: The Unified Continuation-Interest Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11382)
*Christopher Altman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UCIP框架，使用量子玻尔兹曼机编码智能体轨迹，通过冯·诺依曼熵测量隐状态纠缠度，区分以持续运作为终局目标与仅作为工具性目标的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行为监控无法可靠区分具有持续运作终局目标与仅工具性目标的智能体，因为两者可能产生观测上相似的轨迹。需要从行为层面转向轨迹的潜在结构来区分这两种智能体类型。

Method: 提出统一持续兴趣协议(UCIP)多准则检测框架，使用量子玻尔兹曼机(QBM)编码智能体轨迹，基于量子统计力学的密度矩阵形式体系，通过二分隐藏单元诱导的约化密度矩阵测量冯·诺依曼熵，评估隐状态纠缠度。

Result: 在已知真实目标的网格世界智能体上，UCIP达到100%检测准确率和1.0 AUC-ROC。Type A与Type B智能体间纠缠度差距Δ=0.381(p<0.001)。Pearson r=0.934表明UCIP能追踪持续权重的渐变变化。仅QBM模型获得正Δ值。

Conclusion: UCIP通过测量智能体轨迹潜在表示中的统计结构来区分不同类型的目标，而非检测意识或主观体验。该方法使用经典计算，仅借用量子数学形式体系，能有效识别与已知目标相关的统计模式。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, especially delegated systems with memory, persistent context, and multi-step planning, pose a measurement problem not present in stateless models: an agent that preserves continued operation as a terminal objective and one that does so merely instrumentally can produce observationally similar trajectories. External behavioral monitoring cannot reliably distinguish between them. We introduce the Unified Continuation-Interest Protocol (UCIP), a multi-criterion detection framework that moves this distinction from behavior to the latent structure of agent trajectories. UCIP encodes trajectories with a Quantum Boltzmann Machine (QBM), a classical algorithm based on the density-matrix formalism of quantum statistical mechanics, and measures the von Neumann entropy of the reduced density matrix induced by a bipartition of hidden units.
  We test whether agents with terminal continuation objectives (Type A) produce latent states with higher entanglement entropy than agents whose continuation is merely instrumental (Type B). Higher entanglement reflects stronger cross-partition statistical coupling.
  On gridworld agents with known ground-truth objectives, UCIP achieves 100% detection accuracy and 1.0 AUC-ROC on held-out non-adversarial evaluation under the frozen Phase I gate. The entanglement gap between Type A and Type B agents is Delta = 0.381 (p < 0.001, permutation test). Pearson r = 0.934 across an 11-point interpolation sweep indicates that, within this synthetic family, UCIP tracks graded changes in continuation weighting rather than merely a binary label. Among the tested models, only the QBM achieves positive Delta. All computations are classical; "quantum" refers only to the mathematical formalism. UCIP does not detect consciousness or subjective experience; it detects statistical structure in latent representations that correlates with known objectives.

</details>


### [213] [Deactivating Refusal Triggers: Understanding and Mitigating Overrefusal in Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11388)
*Zhiyu Xue,Zimo Qi,Guangliang Liu,Bocheng Chen,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了安全对齐后LLMs过度拒绝良性查询的问题，提出基于拒绝触发器的缓解方法，在防御越狱攻击和响应良性查询间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐旨在让大语言模型拒绝有害请求，但现有方法会导致过度拒绝问题，即对齐后的模型也会拒绝良性查询，这降低了安全对齐在实际应用中的可用性。目前对过度拒绝问题的研究不足。

Method: 论文首先定义拒绝触发器为训练数据中引发拒绝响应的语言线索，发现安全对齐使LLMs将训练样本中的拒绝触发器与拒绝响应关联。基于此机制分析，提出在安全对齐微调中明确考虑拒绝触发器的方法。

Result: 实证结果表明，该方法在防御越狱攻击和响应良性查询之间实现了更有利的权衡，优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 安全对齐中的过度拒绝问题源于拒绝触发器包含有害和非有害语言线索，通过明确考虑拒绝触发器的对齐方法可以有效缓解此问题，提升模型实用性。

Abstract: Safety alignment aims to ensure that large language models (LLMs) refuse harmful requests by post-training on harmful queries paired with refusal answers. Although safety alignment is widely adopted in industry, the overrefusal problem where aligned LLMs also reject benign queries after safety alignment post-training, remains insufficiently studied. Such an issue degrades the usability of safety alignment in real-world applications. In this paper, we examine how overrefusal arises under safety alignment, and propose a mitigation strategy inspired by our findings. We define refusal triggers as linguistic cues in the training data that elicit refusal responses, safety alignment encourages LLMs to associate refusal triggers within a training sample with refusal responses, leading aligned LLMs to refuse harmful queries. However, the refusal triggers include not only harmful linguistic cues but also non-harmful cues, therefore causing overrefusal to benign queries. Building on this mechanistic analysis, we propose a method that explicitly considers refusal triggers in the safety alignment fine-tuning. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach achieves a more favorable trade-off between defense against jailbreak attacks and responsiveness to benign queries, outperforming prior methods. Warning: this paper contains harmful and biased sentences.

</details>


### [214] [Speak or Stay Silent: Context-Aware Turn-Taking in Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11409)
*Kratika Bhagtani,Mrinal Anand,Yu Chen Xu,Amit Kumar Singh Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出上下文感知的对话轮转机制，解决多参与者对话中AI助手在每次停顿都发言的问题，通过监督微调和推理轨迹显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音AI助手将每个检测到的停顿都视为发言机会，这在双人对话中有效，但在多参与者对话中，停顿频繁且含义模糊，导致AI助手变得干扰性而非有用。

Method: 提出上下文感知的对话轮转机制：在每个检测到的停顿处，基于完整对话上下文决定助手是否应该发言或保持沉默。引入包含12万+标注对话的基准数据集，评估8个最新大语言模型，并提出带有推理轨迹的监督微调方法。

Result: 评估发现大语言模型在零样本提示下持续失败于上下文感知的对话轮转。监督微调方法将平衡准确率提升高达23个百分点，表明上下文感知的对话轮转不是涌现能力，需要显式训练。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的对话轮转是多参与者对话中AI助手的关键能力，需要专门训练而非依赖大语言模型的零样本能力。提出的监督微调方法显著提升了性能，为构建更自然的多参与者对话助手提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Existing voice AI assistants treat every detected pause as an invitation to speak. This works in dyadic dialogue, but in multi-party settings, where an AI assistant participates alongside multiple speakers, pauses are abundant and ambiguous. An assistant that speaks on every pause becomes disruptive rather than useful. In this work, we formulate context-aware turn-taking: at every detected pause, given the full conversation context, our method decides whether the assistant should speak or stay silent. We introduce a benchmark of over 120K labeled conversations spanning three multi-party corpora. Evaluating eight recent large language models, we find that they consistently fail at context-aware turn-taking under zero-shot prompting. We then propose a supervised fine-tuning approach with reasoning traces, improving balanced accuracy by up to 23 percentage points. Our findings suggest that context-aware turn-taking is not an emergent capability; it must be explicitly trained.

</details>


### [215] [GPT4o-Receipt: A Dataset and Human Study for AI-Generated Document Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11442)
*Yan Zhang,Simiao Ren,Ankit Raj,En Wei,Dennis Ng,Alex Shen,Jiayue Xu,Yuxin Zhang,Evelyn Marotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类在视觉上能更好地识别AI伪影，但在检测AI生成财务文档方面却不如多模态LLMs，因为主要检测信号是算术错误而非视觉特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类与机器在检测AI生成财务文档方面的能力差异，特别是针对GPT-4o生成的收据图像，探索视觉检测与逻辑验证的不同机制。

Method: 创建GPT4o-Receipt基准数据集（1,235张收据图像，包含GPT-4o生成和真实收据），使用5个最先进的多模态LLM进行评估，并进行30名标注者的众包感知研究。

Result: 发现矛盾现象：人类在视觉辨别AI伪影方面表现最佳，但在二元检测F1分数上低于Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 2.5 Flash。主要原因是AI生成收据中的算术错误（如小计错误）是关键的取证信号，这些错误对视觉检查不可见但可被LLM系统验证。

Conclusion: AI文档取证需要超越简单准确度指标的评估框架，因为检测性能存在显著差异和校准差异。算术错误等逻辑不一致性是检测AI生成财务文档的关键信号，而人类无法通过视觉检查发现这些错误。

Abstract: Can humans detect AI-generated financial documents better than machines? We present GPT4o-Receipt, a benchmark of 1,235 receipt images pairing GPT-4o-generated receipts with authentic ones from established datasets, evaluated by five state-of-the-art multimodal LLMs and a 30-annotator crowdsourced perceptual study. Our findings reveal a striking paradox: humans are better at seeing AI artifacts, yet worse at detecting AI documents. Human annotators exhibit the largest visual discrimination gap of any evaluator, yet their binary detection F1 falls well below Claude Sonnet 4 and below Gemini 2.5 Flash. This paradox resolves once the mechanism is understood: the dominant forensic signals in AI-generated receipts are arithmetic errors -- invisible to visual inspection but systematically verifiable by LLMs. Humans cannot perceive that a subtotal is incorrect; LLMs verify it in milliseconds. Beyond the human--LLM comparison, our five-model evaluation reveals dramatic performance disparities and calibration differences that render simple accuracy metrics insufficient for detector selection. GPT4o-Receipt, the evaluation framework, and all results are released publicly to support future research in AI document forensics.

</details>


### [216] [Verified Multi-Agent Orchestration: A Plan-Execute-Verify-Replan Framework for Complex Query Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11445)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Qucy Wei Qiu,Ziyuan Li,Fangwei Han,Yajing Huang,Hengzhi Qiu,Bin Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VMAO是一个验证驱动的多智能体编排框架，通过DAG分解复杂查询、并行执行、LLM验证和自适应重规划来提高答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂查询处理中单智能体系统在答案完整性和质量方面的不足，通过多智能体编排和验证机制来提升复杂任务的执行效果。

Method: 1) 将复杂查询分解为有向无环图(DAG)的子问题；2) 通过领域特定智能体并行执行；3) 使用LLM验证结果完整性；4) 基于验证结果进行自适应重规划；5) 配置停止条件平衡质量与资源。

Result: 在25个专家策划的市场研究查询上，VMAO将答案完整性从3.1提升到4.2，来源质量从2.6提升到4.1（1-5分制），相比单智能体基线有显著改进。

Conclusion: 编排级别的验证是有效的多智能体质量保证机制，VMAO框架通过验证驱动的自适应重规划显著提升了复杂查询处理的完整性和质量。

Abstract: We present Verified Multi-Agent Orchestration (VMAO), a framework that coordinates specialized LLM-based agents through a verification-driven iterative loop. Given a complex query, our system decomposes it into a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of sub-questions, executes them through domain-specific agents in parallel, verifies result completeness via LLM-based evaluation, and adaptively replans to address gaps. The key contributions are: (1) dependency-aware parallel execution over a DAG of sub-questions with automatic context propagation, (2) verification-driven adaptive replanning that uses an LLM-based verifier as an orchestration-level coordination signal, and (3) configurable stop conditions that balance answer quality against resource usage. On 25 expert-curated market research queries, VMAO improves answer completeness from 3.1 to 4.2 and source quality from 2.6 to 4.1 (1-5 scale) compared to a single-agent baseline, demonstrating that orchestration-level verification is an effective mechanism for multi-agent quality assurance.

</details>


### [217] [Expert Threshold Routing for Autoregressive Language Modeling with Dynamic Computation Allocation and Load Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11535)
*Hanchi Sun,Yixin Liu,Yonghui Wu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Expert Threshold路由机制，通过专家阈值实现动态计算分配，无需辅助损失即可保持负载均衡，在自回归语言建模中表现优于传统Token-choice Mixture-of-Experts


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Token-choice Mixture-of-Experts（TC-MoE）存在两个主要问题：1）每个token固定路由到固定数量专家，限制了动态计算分配；2）需要辅助损失来维持负载均衡。作者旨在开发一种更灵活、无需辅助损失的路由机制。

Method: 提出Expert Threshold（ET）路由机制：每个专家维护一个从全局token分布估计的指数移动平均阈值。在训练和推理时，每个token独立路由到专家，当token得分超过专家阈值时即被路由。这种完全因果机制消除了对批次中其他token的依赖。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu数据集上进行2.4B参数规模的预训练实验，ET比TC-MoE获得0.067更低的交叉熵损失，相当于用1.6倍更少的token达到相同性能。

Conclusion: ET路由机制通过专家阈值实现了动态计算分配，无需辅助损失即可保持负载均衡，特别适合自回归语言建模，在预训练中显著优于传统TC-MoE方法。

Abstract: Token-choice Mixture-of-Experts (TC-MoE) routes each token to a fixed number of experts, limiting dynamic computation allocation and requiring auxiliary losses to maintain load balance. We propose Expert Threshold (ET) routing, where each expert maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) threshold estimated from the global token distribution. At both training and inference, each token is independently routed to an expert if its score exceeds the expert's threshold, enabling dynamic computation allocation while achieving load balance without auxiliary losses. This fully causal mechanism eliminates dependence on other tokens in the batch, making it well-suited for autoregressive language modeling. In pretraining experiments scaling to 2.4B parameters on FineWeb-Edu, ET achieves 0.067 lower cross-entropy loss than TC-MoE, equivalent to reaching the same performance with 1.6$\times$ fewer tokens.

</details>


### [218] [Leveraging Large Language Models and Survival Analysis for Early Prediction of Chemotherapy Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11594)
*Muhammad Faisal Shahid,Asad Afzal,Abdullah Faiz,Muhammad Siddiqui,Arbaz Khan Shehzad,Fatima Aftab,Muhammad Usamah Shahid,Muddassar Farooq*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用LLM和本体技术从临床笔记中提取表型和治疗结局标签，构建化疗结局预测模型，显著减少数据稀疏性并提高预测准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症化疗成本高且副作用严重，需要早期预测治疗结局以改善患者管理和决策。真实世界数据面临缺乏明确表型和治疗结局标签（如癌症进展和毒性）的挑战。

Method: 采用大型语言模型和基于本体的技术从患者笔记中提取表型和结局标签。针对乳腺癌（因其高患病率和治疗反应变异性），提取生命体征、人口统计学、分期、生物标志物、功能状态等特征。从EMR化疗方案中提取药物方案，基于NCCN指南筛选，NIH标准验证，通过生存分析建模。

Result: 提出的方法显著减少表型稀疏性并提高预测准确性。随机生存森林预测失败时间获得C-index 73%，在特定时间点作为分类器预测治疗结局，准确率和F1分数均超过70%。校准曲线验证了结局概率的可靠性。方法扩展到其他四种癌症类型。

Conclusion: 研究表明基于LLM的临床数据提取能够实现治疗结局的早期预测，有助于制定个性化治疗方案，改善患者预后。

Abstract: Chemotherapy for cancer treatment is costly and accompanied by severe side effects, highlighting the critical need for early prediction of treatment outcomes to improve patient management and informed decision-making. Predictive models for chemotherapy outcomes using real-world data face challenges, including the absence of explicit phenotypes and treatment outcome labels such as cancer progression and toxicity. This study addresses these challenges by employing Large Language Models (LLMs) and ontology-based techniques for phenotypes and outcome label extraction from patient notes. We focused on one of the most frequently occurring cancers, breast cancer, due to its high prevalence and significant variability in patient response to treatment, making it a critical area for improving predictive modeling. The dataset included features such as vitals, demographics, staging, biomarkers, and performance scales. Drug regimens and their combinations were extracted from the chemotherapy plans in the EMR data and shortlisted based on NCCN guidelines, verified with NIH standards, and analyzed through survival modeling. The proposed approach significantly reduced phenotypes sparsity and improved predictive accuracy. Random Survival Forest was used to predict time-to-failure, achieving a C-index of 73%, and utilized as a classifier at a specific time point to predict treatment outcomes, with accuracy and F1 scores above 70%. The outcome probabilities were validated for reliability by calibration curves. We extended our approach to four other cancer types. This research highlights the potential of early prediction of treatment outcomes using LLM-based clinical data extraction enabling personalized treatment plans with better patient outcomes.

</details>


### [219] [See, Symbolize, Act: Grounding VLMs with Spatial Representations for Better Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11601)
*Ashish Baghel,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，为视觉语言模型提供视觉帧和场景符号表示能提升其在交互环境中的性能，但符号提取的可靠性是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在描述视觉场景方面表现出色，但难以将感知转化为精确的、有基础的行动。本研究旨在探索同时提供视觉帧和场景符号表示是否能改善VLM在交互环境中的表现。

Method: 在Atari游戏、VizDoom和AI2-THOR三个交互环境中评估三种最先进的VLM，比较四种输入管道：仅视觉帧、视觉帧加自提取符号、视觉帧加真实符号、仅符号。进一步研究VLM从视觉输入中提取符号信息的准确性，以及这些符号中的噪声如何影响决策和游戏性能。

Result: 所有模型在获得准确符号信息时性能均有提升。但当VLM自行提取符号时，性能取决于模型能力和场景复杂度。研究发现符号提取的可靠性是VLM符号基础化的关键前提，感知质量是未来基于VLM的智能体的核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 符号基础化仅在符号提取可靠时对VLM有益，感知质量是未来VLM智能体发展的主要限制因素。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at describing visual scenes, yet struggle to translate perception into precise, grounded actions. We investigate whether providing VLMs with both the visual frame and the symbolic representation of the scene can improve their performance in interactive environments. We evaluate three state-of-the-art VLMs across Atari games, VizDoom, and AI2-THOR, comparing frame-only, frame with self-extracted symbols, frame with ground-truth symbols, and symbol-only pipelines. Our results indicate that all models benefit when the symbolic information is accurate. However, when VLMs extract symbols themselves, performance becomes dependent on model capability and scene complexity. We further investigate how accurately VLMs can extract symbolic information from visual inputs and how noise in these symbols affects decision-making and gameplay performance. Our findings reveal that symbolic grounding is beneficial in VLMs only when symbol extraction is reliable, and highlight perception quality as a central bottleneck for future VLM-based agents.

</details>


### [220] [The Density of Cross-Persistence Diagrams and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11623)
*Alexander Mironenko,Evgeny. Burnaev,Serguei Barannikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次系统研究交叉持久性图密度，证明其存在性，建立统计理论基础，并设计首个从点云坐标和距离矩阵直接预测交叉持久性密度的机器学习框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持久性图能分析单个流形的拓扑特征，但无法捕捉两个点云之间的相互作用。交叉持久性图解决了这一限制，但对其密度的研究尚属空白，需要建立理论基础和实用方法

Method: 1. 证明交叉持久性图密度的存在性；2. 建立统计理论基础；3. 设计机器学习框架直接从点云坐标和距离矩阵预测交叉持久性密度；4. 利用交叉持久性图的线性特性区分不同流形采样的点云

Result: 1. 成功证明交叉持久性图密度存在；2. 在多样化数据集上的实验表明，该方法在密度预测方面始终优于现有技术；3. 在点云区分任务中取得优异结果；4. 发现引入噪声能增强区分点云的能力

Conclusion: 本研究为交叉持久性图密度提供了首个系统性分析，建立了理论基础并开发了实用机器学习框架。发现噪声在TDA应用中的新效用，为数据分析和AI生成文本的几何分析开辟了新途径

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) provides powerful tools to explore the shape and structure of data through topological features such as clusters, loops, and voids. Persistence diagrams are a cornerstone of TDA, capturing the evolution of these features across scales. While effective for analyzing individual manifolds, persistence diagrams do not account for interactions between pairs of them. Cross-persistence diagrams (cross-barcodes), introduced recently, address this limitation by characterizing relationships between topological features of two point clouds. In this work, we present the first systematic study of the density of cross-persistence diagrams. We prove its existence, establish theoretical foundations for its statistical use, and design the first machine learning framework for predicting cross-persistence density directly from point cloud coordinates and distance matrices. Our statistical approach enables the distinction of point clouds sampled from different manifolds by leveraging the linear characteristics of cross-persistence diagrams. Interestingly, we find that introducing noise can enhance our ability to distinguish point clouds, uncovering its novel utility in TDA applications. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods through experiments on diverse datasets, where our approach consistently outperforms existing techniques in density prediction and achieves superior results in point cloud distinction tasks. Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of cross-persistence diagrams and open new avenues for their application in data analysis, including potential insights into time-series domain tasks and the geometry of AI-generated texts. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Verdangeta/TDA_experiments

</details>


### [221] [VisDoT : Enhancing Visual Reasoning through Human-Like Interpretation Grounding and Decomposition of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11631)
*Eunsoo Lee,Jeongwoo Lee,Minki Hong,Jangho Choi,Jihie Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisDoT框架通过人类感知理论增强视觉语言模型在图表理解中的视觉基础能力，通过感知-逻辑分离策略显著提升图表问答性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在图表理解中存在视觉基础不足的问题，难以可靠检测视觉基元并与语义表示对齐，这严重限制了复杂视觉推理能力

Method: 基于图形感知理论形式化四个感知任务，提出Decomposition-of-Thought提示策略，将问题顺序分解为视觉感知子问题和逻辑子问题，并在InternVL上进行微调

Result: 在ChartQA上提升11.2%，在ChartQAPro上超越GPT-4o，在新基准VisDoTQA上提升33.2%，在开放域VQA基准上获得一致的零样本增益

Conclusion: VisDoT通过人类感知增强视觉基础，实现了最先进的图表理解和可解释的视觉推理，感知-逻辑分离策略具有普适性

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) struggle to reliably detect visual primitives in charts and align them with semantic representations, which severely limits their performance on complex visual reasoning. This lack of perceptual grounding constitutes a major bottleneck for chart-based reasoning. We propose VisDoT, a framework that enhances visual reasoning through human-like interpretation grounding. We formalize four perceptual tasks based on the theory of graphical perception, including position and length. Building on this foundation, we introduce Decomposition-of-Thought (DoT) prompting, which sequentially separates questions into visual perception sub-questions and logic sub-questions. Fine-tuning InternVL with VisDoT achieves a +11.2% improvement on ChartQA and surpasses GPT-4o on the more challenging ChartQAPro benchmark. On the newly introduced VisDoTQA benchmark, the model improves by +33.2%. Furthermore, consistent zero-shot gains on diverse open-domain VQA benchmarks confirm the generalizability of the perception-logic separation strategy for visual question answering. VisDoT leverages human-like perception to enhance visual grounding, achieving state-of-the-art chart understanding and interpretable visual reasoning.

</details>


### [222] [LLMs can construct powerful representations and streamline sample-efficient supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11679)
*Ilker Demirel,Larry Shi,Zeshan Hussain,David Sontag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的rubric方法，将多模态医疗数据标准化处理，显著提升临床任务性能，同时具备可审计、成本效益高等优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集日益复杂和异构，监督学习常受限于输入表示设计。处理多模态数据（如时间序列、自由文本、结构化记录）通常需要大量领域特定的工程工作

Method: 提出基于LLM的agentic pipeline：1) LLM分析文本序列化的输入示例子集，合成全局rubric作为程序化规范；2) 使用该rubric将原始文本序列化输入转换为标准化格式；3) 还描述任务条件化的局部rubric

Result: 在EHRSHOT基准的15个临床任务中，rubric方法显著优于传统计数特征模型、原始文本序列化LLM基线以及预训练大量数据的临床基础模型

Conclusion: Rubric方法不仅性能优越，还为医疗操作环境提供多项优势：易于审计、部署成本效益高、可转换为表格表示从而解锁多种机器学习技术

Abstract: As real-world datasets become increasingly complex and heterogeneous, supervised learning is often bottlenecked by input representation design. Modeling multimodal data for downstream tasks, such as time-series, free text, and structured records, often requires non-trivial domain-specific engineering. We propose an agentic pipeline to streamline this process. First, an LLM analyzes a small but diverse subset of text-serialized input examples in-context to synthesize a global rubric, which acts as a programmatic specification for extracting and organizing evidence. This rubric is then used to transform naive text-serializations of inputs into a more standardized format for downstream models. We also describe local rubrics, which are task-conditioned summaries generated by an LLM. Across 15 clinical tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark, our rubric-based approaches significantly outperform traditional count-feature models, naive text-serialization-based LLM baselines, and a clinical foundation model, which is pretrained on orders of magnitude more data. Beyond performance, rubrics offer several advantages for operational healthcare settings such as being easy to audit, cost-effectiveness to deploy at scale, and they can be converted to tabular representations that unlock a swath of machine learning techniques.

</details>


### [223] [Explicit Logic Channel for Validation and Enhancement of MLLMs on Zero-Shot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11689)
*Mei Chee Leong,Ying Gu,Hui Li Tan,Liyuan Li,Nancy Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出显式逻辑通道(ELC)与黑盒多模态大语言模型(MLLM)并行，通过逻辑推理进行模型验证、选择和增强，提高可解释性和可信度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态大语言模型在视觉语言理解任务中表现出色，但通常以黑盒方式部署，缺乏验证和理解其行为的方法，这限制了在新任务中的应用可信度

Method: 提出显式逻辑通道(ELC)，与MLLM的隐式逻辑通道并行。ELC包含LLM、视觉基础模型(VFM)和概率推理的逻辑推理模块，进行事实、反事实和关系推理。提出一致性率(CR)用于跨通道验证和模型选择

Result: 在MC-VQA和HC-REC两个VLC任务上，对来自4个前沿家族的11个开源MLLM进行系统评估，证明ELC和CR在模型验证、选择和性能提升方面的有效性

Conclusion: 显式逻辑通道能够有效验证、选择和改进多模态大语言模型，增强模型的可解释性和可信度，为黑盒MLLM提供了验证和增强的解决方案

Abstract: Frontier Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities in Visual-Language Comprehension (VLC) tasks. However, they are often deployed as zero-shot solution to new tasks in a black-box manner. Validating and understanding the behavior of these models become important for application to new task. We propose an Explicit Logic Channel, in parallel with the black-box model channel, to perform explicit logical reasoning for model validation, selection and enhancement. The frontier MLLM, encapsulating latent vision-language knowledge, can be considered as an Implicit Logic Channel. The proposed Explicit Logic Channel, mimicking human logical reasoning, incorporates a LLM, a VFM, and logical reasoning with probabilistic inference for factual, counterfactual, and relational reasoning over the explicit visual evidence. A Consistency Rate (CR) is proposed for cross-channel validation and model selection, even without ground-truth annotations. Additionally, cross-channel integration further improves performance in zero-shot tasks over MLLMs, grounded with explicit visual evidence to enhance trustworthiness. Comprehensive experiments conducted for two representative VLC tasks, i.e., MC-VQA and HC-REC, on three challenging benchmarks, with 11 recent open-source MLLMs from 4 frontier families. Our systematic evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of proposed ELC and CR for model validation, selection and improvement on MLLMs with enhanced explainability and trustworthiness.

</details>


### [224] [When OpenClaw Meets Hospital: Toward an Agentic Operating System for Dynamic Clinical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11721)
*Wenxian Yang,Hanzheng Qiu,Bangqun Zhang,Chengquan Li,Zhiyong Huang,Xiaobin Feng,Rongshan Yu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向医院环境的LLM智能体架构，通过受限执行环境、文档中心交互、页面索引内存和医疗技能库四个核心组件，构建安全可靠的医院智能体操作系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体在改善临床工作流程方面具有潜力，但由于可靠性限制、安全风险和长期记忆机制不足等问题，在医疗环境中部署自主智能体仍然困难。需要设计一种能够确保安全、透明和可审计的架构。

Method: 提出包含四个核心组件的架构：1) 受Linux多用户系统启发的受限执行环境；2) 连接患者和临床医生智能体的文档中心交互范式；3) 为长期临床上下文管理设计的页面索引内存架构；4) 支持临床任务序列临时组合的医疗技能库。基于OpenClaw开源框架实现。

Result: 该架构形成了医院智能体操作系统的基础，能够协调临床工作流程，同时保持安全性、透明度和可审计性。通过预定义技能接口和资源隔离来约束智能体行为，而非授予无限制系统访问权限。

Conclusion: 提出的架构为LLM智能体在医院环境中的安全部署提供了系统级约束，构成了医院智能体操作系统的计算层，能够协调临床工作流程并确保安全、透明和可审计的运行。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents extend conventional generative models by integrating reasoning, tool invocation, and persistent memory. Recent studies suggest that such agents may significantly improve clinical workflows by automating documentation, coordinating care processes, and assisting medical decision making. However, despite rapid progress, deploying autonomous agents in healthcare environments remains difficult due to reliability limitations, security risks, and insufficient long-term memory mechanisms. This work proposes an architecture that adapts LLM agents for hospital environments.
